WAPPINGER CREEK NEAR WAPPINGERS FALLS NY, USGS site 01372500 (USGS-01372500), stream in Dutchess County, New York, draining 181 square miles. Daily means from 1928-08-07 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
